# Tripo asset list (Phase 3 deliverable, revised 2026-10-11)

**Status:** PROPOSED, generated from the 40-chapter story, scripts and level plans (`docs/Story/`). Every asset appears once. This replaces the first 279-asset list: it now covers each chapter's own cast, set, props, enemies, boss, vehicles and a modular kit of dressing pieces. `[IMPLIED]` marks assets not explicitly described by the owner (most of them).

Negative prompt for every entry: **no background, no base, no floating parts**.

Tripo prompt format: `[ART STYLE], [subject], [key features], [main material], [texture detail], game-ready, clean topology, A-pose (if rigged)`.
Art style: `anime cel-shaded stylized 3D, thick ink outlines, cyberpunk 2009 Neo Tokyo`.

Columns: # | Asset | First chapter | Description | Rig? (Y/N, A-pose/T-pose) | Tripo prompt | Recommended settings | Polygon target (LOD0) | Production notes. The level builder places a labelled greybox stand-in for each asset in its first chapter's map (`Tools/story_assets.json`).

## Recommended settings and polygon targets — 2026-10-11

**Status: WORKING technical recommendations.** These are starting targets for the existing proposed assets, not approved character designs or measured performance limits. Each asset below has its own settings, polygon target and production note. Existing names, descriptions, rig flags, prompts and `[IMPLIED]` status are preserved.

- **Workflow:** prefer the existing approved image references when available; otherwise retain the listed text prompt. Use Build & Refine to review geometry before texturing. “Standard-detail” and “High-detail” describe the desired base fidelity, not a guaranteed UI preset. Choose the corresponding available quality setting; reserve Ultra for a source that needs it.
- **Topology and count:** set Retopology → Polygon Count to the listed number. Quads are recommended for deforming character source meshes; triangles for static/mechanical runtime meshes. A quad usually becomes two triangles on export. The row explicitly states its unit; mixed topology and export cleanup can change the result. Check the exported count. Do not enter the parenthesized triangle estimate as the quad target.
- **Smart Low Poly:** on for most rigid objects; off for deforming/high-detail character source meshes to protect face, joint and cloth detail. Quad output still needs manual edge-flow and skin-weight checks. Use manual primitives/decals/VFX where indicated; a “no Tripo mesh” row has no generation polygon setting.
- **Textures:** row resolutions (1K = 1024, 2K = 2048, 4K = 4096 pixels per map) are delivery targets. Select the closest available texture option, then resize/bake in the DCC if necessary. Use Remove Lighting/de-lighting where offered, with PBR maps as a material source; apply the project's cel-shaded lighting and outlines in Unreal. Preserve intended emission/transparency manually. Flat grey rows need no generated textures. Use private visibility where available.
- **Scope of each target:** per asset at LOD0 unless the row says per module, member, phase or carriage. A location/set is assembled from modules; its target is not a whole-scene allowance. A crowd budget is per distinct member, multiplied by visible instances. Separate props/weapons are additional to the character budget. For split character generation, allocate the total across all parts rather than applying the full character target to every part.
- **Runtime delivery:** these are cross-platform starting budgets, not a guarantee of mobile performance. Begin with LOD1 ≈ 50% and LOD2 ≈ 25% of LOD0 triangles, preserving faces and silhouettes; profile visible instances, materials, skinning, transparency and texture memory on target devices. Mobile texture delivery can reduce character/vehicle 4K maps to 2K and small 2K props to 1K.
- **Existing owner-directed character-part settings:** prior H3.1/Ultra/8K/Quad 40,000 instructions remain specific to that recorded workflow. The whole-asset recommendations here do not change those earlier generation settings or authorize generation/retries/spending.

Tripo control names and workflow were checked against the [official Studio tutorial](https://www.tripo3d.ai/blog/tripo-studio-tutorial-english) and [official model documentation](https://developers.tripo3d.ai/en/docs/generation-image-to-model) on 2026-10-11. Numeric budgets are asset-specific recommendations from this review, not vendor specifications. Exact options in the owner's current Studio session remain unverified.
